In the same way there is carried out also the process of cognition as the movement of the specific (gnoseological) form of reality at the level and in the terms of human thinking. As for the question of the cognizability of the surrounding world, then in the final account it will inevitably be necessary to come to the understanding of the circumstance that the world — this is not a book and all the more not a rock or a boulder-stone in its immutability, but something unceasingly "making itself", reproducing itself, changing itself in the regime of real time; and a similar question in a correct formulation must be posed not in relation to the cognizability of something given and existing from the age in its constancy, but in relation to the possibility of the foresight and anticipation of the newly arising forms (including the consequences, results, events, configurations, situations) of the developing reality.

The levels of development are often inseparable, undetachable from each other for the reason that the contentful constituent, say, of one level can at one and the same time (in interconnection) turn out to be the formal constituent for another level and vice versa. And also for the reason that the contentful constituent of one level necessarily represents a new relation {content/form} for another level. The same thing should be said also about any formal constituent. But the main obstacle on the path of the clear understanding of the course of the process of development and of its exact formulations consists in the fact that the natural boundary between the components-constituents "content" and "form" in their relation is hardly distinguishable, and at times simply not perceptible (for the time being). Thus, the ingrowing of the levels-forms into each other and their growing out of one another is precisely the cause of the impossibility of their separation in a "pure" form, as well as of the intricacy of the processes and of the difficulty of the expression (explanation) of the unexplored, and in some sense irrational, through the comprehended rational. In such a connection one must always take into account the dual nature of the world, and especially in the cases when the matter concerns the examination and investigation of intricate (and precisely therefore complex) questions. This thought is far from new, we simply often forget about it. And in order to come out onto the correct solution of the fundamental problems, without the dialectical approach it is already in no way possible to get by. The striving to express the new abstract content through the preceding more concrete forms by virtue of the effect of the winding of content, described and conditioned (the speech is about the effect of winding) by the formulation of the principle of self-development, leads to the search of a new form for the new content, which is the continuation of all the other contentful forms present in the linkage or in the aggregate. This form includes in itself in the "sublated", concentrated form the preceding manifestations of the essence of the object and simultaneously is their continuation, expansion, and superstructure with the "inclusions" of some peculiarities (differences) not present until then in the preceding forms. Therefore the main thing that one should catch in this process is not the "reflection" of one in the other (not the doubling or multiplication of essences), but the transformation, the transformation of the available linkage of the preceding forms into a new aggregate form, and moreover not equivalent to the simple sum of the forms, but qualitatively different from it. The life and existence of man as the subject of cognition — this is the movement of the contentful constituent through the forms-levels in the direction of the acquisition of the most contentful form of such a movement, which is precisely what is affirmed by the formulation of the principle of selfdevelopment. In words we, as the embodiment of the predominantly conservative component-constituent of the relation {change/basis}, i.e., of the "basis", of course, can deny that at every moment by each our action, by each breath in and out, by each word and deed (being simultaneously the

personification also of the second component-constituent of the relation, i.e., of the "change") we participate in the search and creation of the most contentful forms of our existence and development; however, in fact everything indicates that there is no alternative to this process. § 16. The systematization and refinement of some notions and definitions in the light of our representations about the development and cognition of reality Let us attempt to systematize what has been set forth above, breaking through, as before, through the veil of delusions and confusion in the notions and representations about the subject of our conversation. The truthfulness of a form is determined by its contentfulness; hence the criterion of truth or of the truthfulness of the forms is served by their contentfulness. The more contentful the form, the more truthful it is. The internal contentfulness of a form reveals itself by diverse external manifestations, executions, embodiments. From time immemorial man observes and fixes the multitude of manifestations of the internal contentfulness of the forms, such as: beauty and elegance, simplicity, harmony, laconism, systemicity, economy, noncontradictoriness, self-sufficiency, etc. For the establishment and confirmation of the truthfulness of any form all these manifestationsexecutions of its contentfulness must pass one and the same procedure of assessment — interpretation, — consisting in the determination of the possibility and admissibility of the substitution of the contents of the investigated forms by the contents of as large as possible a number of concrete and abstract forms, the truthfulness of which has already been confirmed and does not raise doubts. The matter remains only for the fact that with respect to each concrete manifestation or execution of contentfulness one should find (reveal, select) its own specific standards for the correct determination of the "quantity" of this contentfulness and the expression of it in conventional units. The more such manifestations falling into the field of vision of the human consciousness, the more complex, all-embracing, and informative the criterion of the assessment of the truthfulness of the forms turns out to be; in other words, if all the manifestations of contentfulness are gathered together, then there will be obtained the combined criterion of the all-round assessment of the truthfulness of any form, — by the aggregate of the manifestations of the contentfulness of this form. Hence, apparently, proceeds also the intuitive striving of a number of researchers to introduce a certain complex criterion of the assessment of truth; the striving itself is quite natural and justified, all the more so that in many fields of human activity, conditioned and generated by the different manifestations of the contentfulness of all possible forms, the criteria of the

assessment of the truthfulness of the forms have already been worked out and exist, in others — they are still to be worked out. But with all the perspectiveness of the indicated idea one should nevertheless stop on the peculiarities of its interpretation and embodiment. "We suppose, — write the authors, — that the synthesis of the existing conceptions of truth is possible. The core of the general theory of truth can become the conception of correspondence, in connection with the fact that only it discloses the essence of true knowledge. According to the words of Descartes, who clearly and most briefly formulated the main idea of the theory of correspondence, "truth... means the correspondence of the thought to the object". ... Without the correlation with the object the notion of truth has no sense, there is no objectless truth, no truth about nothing" [4, p. 6— 7]. On the example of the cited quotation let us examine the confusion in the representations, about which we have declared above. The matter is that in this fragment of the article (as also in the multitude of publications on this theme) there is compared the not quite comparable, similar, say, to the attempt of the establishment of the correspondence between meters and seconds. Despite the fact that both the one and the other "grow" from one root as the components-constituents of the integral relation {time/space}, nevertheless they are not equivalent and not identical to each other, just as the components of the primordial relation {content/form} are not identical and not interchangeable. Here there is evident the attempt to correlate and identify the "formal" and "contentful" beginnings of reality, which by and large is incorrect by virtue of their natural incompatibility. The truthfulness of knowledge is determined by its contentfulness. The fuller, the higher the contentfulness of knowledge, the more truthful, more reliable it is. As we see, the objectness here is generally beside the point. The necessity of the "correlation with the object" appears not in connection with the assessment of the truthfulness of knowledge, but in connection with the assessment of the fullness or the "quantity" of its contentfulness, which (the assessment) is carried out, as has been said above, by means of the procedure of practice or the technology of interpretation, i.e., by the comparison and substitution (as complete as possible) of the content of the abstract form of knowledge by the contents of the more concrete forms of sensations and/or by the contents of the abstract forms of thinking, the truthfulness of which has already been confirmed and does not raise doubts. And only on the basis of the established contentfulness of knowledge can one speak about its truthfulness. The truthfulness of this or that form, thus, has no direct relation to objectness (if only for the reason that the confirmations of truthfulness are equally required by both the abstract and the empirical forms or the forms of sensations, closely connected with the thing forms). The truthfulness of the abstract forms is correlated with objectness only indirectly — through the procedure of the assessment and establishment of the fullness of the contentfulness of the abstract forms.

For this same reason the verbal turns of speech of the type "the correspondence of the thought to the object" (in the formulation of Descartes) or "the correlation of the notion with the object" (in the modern formulation) from ancient times and up to this day are used incorrectly with respect to the procedure of the search of truth or the establishment of the truthfulness of knowledge. One should not forget that the subjective human knowledge about a real object serves as the expression of the objective content of the form of the object, already mediated by the form of human sensations and by the form of human thinking. Therefore only the contents of the form of sensations and of the form of thinking can be compared, correlated, tested for correspondence. And only on the basis of the revealed (in the process of comparison and correlation) to some extent conjugacy, correspondence, equivalence, or "coincidence" of these contents, only then can one make the conclusion about some commonality and continuity of their forms, and, consequently, about the objectivity of the contents of the forms of sensations and thinking, supplementing, deepening, and developing the objective content of the form of the object itself. By virtue of the indicated circumstance the objective content of the form of the object appears already in the quality of the objective content of the generalized multilevel aggregate (symbiosis) of all the three conjugated successive forms of the different levels (the form of thingness, the form of sensations, and the form of thinking). Finally, the content of the above-mentioned aggregate of forms, manifested by means of the form of thinking, with the objectivity certified (with the help of the procedure of practice or the technology of interpretation) (i.e., with the highest contentfulness) can lay claim to the status of true knowledge about the object. Thus, the content of the abstract form (of knowledge, thought, etc.) requires the "correlation" or "correspondence" not with the object (objectness, being, etc.), but with the content of the thing form, and moreover mediated by the form of sensations. And already on the basis of the mentioned correlation and the discovered correspondence (correspondence) or continuity of the compared contents the conclusion is made about the presence in the abstract form of objective content, and, consequently, about the truthfulness of the abstract form. In connection with the remarks expressed here in the address of the fragment of the article [4] I would like nevertheless to note that the article, in our opinion, is still topical, but there are some typical inaccuracies which cannot but be paid attention to in the context of the questions under consideration. Continuing to think straightforwardly and ignoring without exaggeration the leading role and significance of the content-form relations in the development (and cognition) of reality, we shall never separate the wheat from the chaff and shall be marking time still unforgivably long. In order to truly penetrate into the essence of any question and to make it out thoroughly, one must consider this question necessarily through the prism of the content-form transformations (unfortunately, this appeal so far is a voice

crying in the wilderness). This is all the more necessary for the reason that we live in conditions when the time for the anticipation of the development of events and the correct response reaction to the so-called challenges is catastrophically shrinking. Equally there is no doubt in the fact that the prepared and uncompromising reader will discover in the text of the present article also the inevitable flaws, which would be very good and absolutely normal, for only by the path of the successive approximations can one approach the ever more exact and "exhaustive" expression of the objective (true) content of the developing reality. Further one should necessarily once again emphasize that, despite the difference of the external manifestations of the contentfulness of the forms, the technology (or procedure, or way) of the assessment of the truthfulness of any forms remains universal and standard — this is interpretation, the particular case of which is practice. This technology consists in the revelation of the possibility and admissibility of the substitution of the contents of the investigated forms by the contents of as large as possible a number of concrete and abstract forms (with the already established and confirmed truthfulness); i.e., the technology of interpretation is reduced in the final account to the search or working out of the so-called standards or conventional units, by means of which one can express or present the "quantity" or the fullness of the assessed contentfulness of the investigated forms. The essence of this technology is always one — the expression of the unknown through the known, of the unusual through the habitual, of the uncognized through the cognized, of the incomprehensible through the comprehended, of the irrational through the rational, of the indefinite through the definite, of the more abstract and less concrete through the less abstract and more concrete, etc., etc. Everything uncognized and unexplored is assessed, tested, and expressed through the already available and existing, the truthfulness of which is established and tested by time. Nothing new here neither man nor nature will be able to "invent" any more, since both the process of development (of anything whatsoever) and the process of cognition (of anything whatsoever) have always submitted, submit, and will submit to the universal principle of self-development. Herewith it has no essential significance whether the particular is expressed through the general or vice versa, — the main thing is that as the result of the described procedure everything until then unexplored and indefinite acquires a more or less finished (balanced for the time being) form of something tested, proved, achieved, and comprehended. However, there are also other points of view [9]. The impossibility to wholly or all-embracingly and exhaustively cover the "making and developing" by the existing and achieved both conditions and generates the approximateness, inexactness or, so to say, the "chronic" unfinishedness of the expression or coverage of the content by the form in the course of the interaction of these two constituents. There necessarily

remains something contentful (or some its share), uncovered and not yielding to the coverage by any form, with whatever "zeal" the latter would lay claim to the very perfection. The spiral, as also the comma — this is a circle "with development" or a point "with a tail", foretelling always a new turn of events and a step into the insufficiently definite and not fully explored. It is also necessary to note that with the described approach to the assessment and establishment of truth or the truthfulness of the different forms of knowledge it has no significance whatsoever — whether this is humanitarian knowledge (from the field of literature, journalism, politics, art, etc.) or natural-scientific (technical, physical, mathematical, etc.), since falsehood and falsity are objective categories, the essence of which consists in the low contentfulness (and, consequently, in the untruthfulness) of the contents of their forms, which one way or another, sooner or later will be refuted and rejected by the principle of self-development, indicating the objective movement-striving to the highest contentfulness (and hence, also to the truthfulness) of all without exception the developing forms. The sooner man realizes this, the farther from the edge of the abyss (i.e., from the threat of the disappearance of humanity as a definite level-form of reality) he will (re)move. Thus, the truthfulness of any form is determined by its contentfulness; the most truthful are the forms possessing the highest contentfulness. Proceeding from this, the criterion of the truthfulness of the forms is served by their contentfulness, whereas practice or in the broader sense interpretation — this is only the universal technology, procedure, or way of the assessment of the fullness or the "quantity" of the contentfulness of the investigated forms. If one unites just what has been said into one phrase, then there will be obtained such a more or less compact expression: the criterion of truth or of the truthfulness of the investigated forms — this is the contentfulness of these forms, the fullness or the "quantity" of which is established by the possibility and admissibility of the substitution (at least mental) of the contents of the investigated forms by the contents of as large as possible (in the limit — infinite) a number of concrete and abstract forms with the already established and confirmed truthfulness. With respect to the determination of the criterion of the scientificity of knowledge (i.e., of the objectivity of the content of the abstract forms of knowledge) one can say the following. The criterion of the scientificity of knowledge (or the criterion of the objectivity of the content of the abstract forms of knowledge) — this is again the contentfulness of these forms, the fullness or the "quantity" of which is established by the possibility and admissibility of the substitution (at least

mental) of the contents of the abstract forms by the contents of a certain conditionally-limited number of concrete and abstract forms with the already established and confirmed truthfulness. From the position of our approach the main functions of science — the explanatory and the prognostic — can be approximately determined and characterized in the following way. Explanation — this is the interpretation (expression, substitution, replacement) of the contents of the unknown, incomprehensible, unusual, non-standard abstract forms (judgments, constructions, structures, theories, conceptions, formulas, etc.) by the contents of the known concrete, comprehensible, habitual, standard forms. Forecasting — this is the anticipation of the formation, appearance, or discovery of the new forms of reality (as well as the foretelling of the direction of the changes of the existing its forms) with the help of and on the basis of the "invented", generated, created, formulated generalized abstract forms (judgments, theories, conceptions, hypotheses, constructions, structures, formulas, etc.), the objectivity of the contents (i.e., the contentfulness) of which either has already been established and confirmed, or is in the stage of the assessment and confirmation by the technology of interpretation. The very important criteria of the scientificity of knowledge — the reproducibility and repeatability of the results of the empirical investigations — in the terms of our approach mean the admissibility of the multiple interpretation (expression, substitution, replacement) with the identical result of the contents of the investigated empirical forms by the contents of the similar empirical forms, the truthfulness of which has already been established and does not raise doubts. The logic of the exposition of the research approach expounded here requires also the corresponding interpretation and refinement of the term "adequacy" from the position of our representations about the development and cognition. And again we "dance from the stove". The philosophical dictionaries determine adequacy in the following way: "In the theory of cognition the term 'adequate' serves for the designation of the correct reproduction in the representations, notions, and judgments of the objective connections and relations of actuality. In this sense truth is determined as the adequacy of thinking to being" [19, p. 13]. By this definition we are again attempted to be convinced of the correctness of the comparison of the incomparable and the comparison of the incomparable. Contrary to the cited definition, adequacy has no direct relation to being, just as truth has no direct relation to objectness (as has

been indicated above). The adequacy of thinking in the given case means the high contentfulness of the forms-products of this very thinking, which has already passed the assessment or test by interpretation, as a result of which the mentioned forms-products are recognized as true. Thereby the term adequate designates and characterizes the content of the true form of thinking, maximally corresponding (i.e., successive and continuing by itself) to the objective content of the form of thingness (or to the objective content of being), mediated by the form of sensations. And truth, as has been determined earlier, — this is the form (but the form as the aggregate of the forms of thingness, sensations, and thinking), fulfilled with the highest (objective) contentfulness. And the one and the other — deeply interconnected notions. The inadequate thinking, operating with the content of the untrue forms, is punished by "non-being" ahead of time and prematurely. The adequate notion, the adequate image, the adequate representation, the adequate theory, the adequate doctrine, etc. — this is all about the objective content of the diverse true abstract forms-products of thinking. As we see, the term "adequate" is applicable only for the assessment, designation, and statement of the fullness of the contentfulness of the uppermost layer or the summit of the pyramid (i.e., of the human consciousness), in the foundation of which lie the more primitive materialthing and plant-animal levels; at the same time, one should clearly realize that without the linkage and interengagement with these more "down-toearth" fundamental levels that "summit" level of the human consciousness simply does not exist. The adequate thinking — this is the thinking operating with the content of the true forms (the most contentful abstract constructions and structures), tested for the fullness of contentfulness by the procedure (technology) of interpretation. The latter means that the highest contentfulness (and hence, also the truthfulness) of such forms has already been confirmed and certified by the numerous concrete examples and factual material (including the abstract forms tested and approved for the use). The inadequacy of thinking is punished by the premature (early) termination of the development of the form of man and humanity (by the retardation of development, the "tearing apart" of the form into parts, the absorption and assimilation by the competitors). One of the signs of the inadequacy of the thinking of the modern man is the neglect of dialectics. The regress of a form — this is already the indicator that it is "torn apart", both from within and from without, into parts by the other, more active

levels. The form that has stopped in its development is "picked up" by the neighboring levels, assimilated (wholly or in parts) and acquires thereby a new impulse for its own further development. Integrally, harmoniously, capably, effectively, actually, and truly is that which is filled with objective content, which is the most contentful at the given moment and under the given circumstances. The continuity of the forms presupposes the smooth docking, the smooth ingrowing, the smooth transition from the contents of the forms of the thingpractical level to the contents of the forms-products of the level of thinking (which are the abstract representations and images). However, the determining here all the same, in the final account, turns out to be the objective content of the thing-practical activity, i.e., the contents of the forms of the thing level (mediated by the forms of sensations), since they are more fundamental in the multilevel structures of the forms of man and humanity. The abstract forms-products of the subjective thinking (i.e., knowledge in the form of representations, images, notions, theories, doctrines, etc.) and the more concrete forms preceding them are capable of being built and are built into the non-contradictory-successive sequence or chain of forms only then, when they are endowed with objective content, when they are united by the common component, which is the objective content of the developing reality. In the indicated sense the objective content of the developing reality — this is the identity to the infinite movement to the exhaustive expression of the content of reality by the developing forms of this very same reality. In its turn, the highest contentfulness of knowledge and of its forms (i.e., the filledness of the abstract forms with objective content) and, correspondingly, the adequacy of the perception of reality (the approximate, but as complete as possible coincidence or correspondence of the contents of the abstract forms of thinking and of the forms of sensations) ensure the success of the human activity and serve as the condition of the survival and stable development of humanity. In the modern literature (and all the more in the publications of the past) the notion of adequacy is very weakly, or more precisely, practically in no way tied up with the contentfulness of the forms. Meanwhile the solution of the task of the establishment and detailing of such a connection will inevitably lead to the numerous discoveries in the field of medicine, biology, cybernetics, and other spheres of the scientific activity. With respect to man the contentfulness in the face of such manifestations of the organism as health, longevity, adaptation abilities and possibilities, resource, intellect, etc., must be investigated in the closest interconnection with the adequacy of the thinking, perception, behavior of man (and of the other higher representatives of the animal world), if the researcher is set on the obtaining of the original (breakthrough) results in the course of his investigations.

§ 17. The truth about lies The present section is devoted to the illustration of the material set forth above by some concrete examples and considerations. As has already been noted in § 3, the modern mass media, controlled by the collective West, are devoid of doubts as to the "exceptional" justice of their endless attempts to attain unseemly goals by deliberate injections of false information, by force pressure, and by the manipulation of the consciousness of the man in the street, and by all possible means to push through the superiority of their own contentfulness, i.e., of their own "unsurpassed" understanding of truth or verity, mixed with false premises and foundations. In confirmation of what has been said let us cite the frankly cynical opinion on this score (in an article with the derisive title "The Rabies of Russia") of one of the modern propagandists of such opinions, who has "settled" on the screens of Russian TV: "... Recently we saw the manifestation of the American 'right of the strong' in all its glory — the murder of the Iranian general Qasem Soleimani. ... Yes, such an application of the 'right of the strong' is outrageous, dishonest, and often unlawful. ... But the fact remains a fact: the 'right of the strong' is an inevitable, even natural, I would say, part of world politics. Whether anyone likes it or not, one must accept such a phenomenon as a given. Similar to the Darwinian natural selection, the 'international order' has existed from time immemorial, and so it will always be to one degree or another. The right of the strong — this is one of the most vivid and harsh manifestations of 'realpolitik'" [1]. From time immemorial the Anglo-Saxon world has been consistent in its main goals — to impose its own scenario of the development of events contrary to their objective course and adequate perception. The AngloSaxons are obsessed with the belief that by a simple "wishing", by pressing, by the inculcation of a certain oversimplification (i.e., by the refusal to take into account the multitude of interacting factors) one can artificially break the naturally developing contentful process. By their standards, the thing-objectmaterial in its monetary expression will always prevail and overcome everything else. For the assessment of the weight and authority of the position occupied in the cited article one must answer a simple question: is the contentfulness of the theory of "social Darwinism" high at the present stage of development? Undoubtedly, something "long-playing" remains from this theory to this very day (man, for example, will never fully free himself from the animal principle present in him, if only because he has "come out" of this principle). At the same time, any schoolboy today knows that "wild" nature is represented by

only a narrow stratum, an intermediate level in the universal human nature, alongside a whole series of newly superstructured levels, based on the taking into account of such qualities of the developed man as his dignity, readiness for cooperation and mutual aid, humanism, etc. Undoubtedly, the level of "wildness" is also represented in the linkage of all the other levels of the multilevel structure of man and at times tramples under itself the less aggressive by their nature "intellectual" ("herbivorous") levels, but it is also beyond doubt that its specific weight in the general aggregate of levels at the present stage of development is considerably smaller than in the epoch of Ch. Darwin. And to reduce the whole objective contentfulness of the existing linkage-aggregate of levels in the general structure of the modern man and his thinking to this separate, very "down-to-earth" ("carnivorous") level — this is a simple-minded delusion or a deliberate lie. The justice of this point of view is confirmed by the centuries-old wisdom of our people (or by the somewhat paraphrased saying of Alexander Nevsky): not in force is god (victory, success, perspective, goal, etc.), but in truth, in verity ... But to the above-named cohort of people with colonial thinking this cannot be explained. The speech is about people not simply of another discourse and not even of another civilizational order, but about "offspring" and exponents of another (opposite) principle of reality, with whom to speak about the priority of harmony or of the objectively existing tendency to the achievement of a certain balance of the constituents-levels in the multilevel human nature is so far meaningless. They are convinced that the hypertrophied technological constituent in their state structure guarantees them complete success and victory over the whole world. What nonsense! On this soil they absolutize their significance and greatness; in reality, however, they are sick with their imaginary exclusivity and see nothing further than their own nose. All the more problematic for themselves. The low-contentful (primitive) consciousness has a chance never to reach the critically necessary level and to turn out to be a dead-end branch of the development of humanity: gentlemen Neanderthals, where are you? Hello! Fortunately or unfortunately, but the objective truth exists; at any given moment it is one (out of the multitude of similar approximations to the absolute truth), and ever more insistently the requirement to determine oneself with this question "in an adult way" shows through. The neglect of the objective truth is fraught with the loss of humanity as a species or as one of the levels of reality; at stake is the fate of humanity, no more and no less. The main stake of today's "masters of life" and of the mass media subject to them — on the low "down-to-earth" instincts of man, i.e., on his "dehumanization": the accent is made on the stimulation and increase of the contentfulness of only the most primitive forms-levels (in the composition of the whole aggregate of the levels-constituents of the modern man), and, in particular, of the simplest forms of sensations and behavioral reactions of the

animal level. The artless content of the indicated forms is "sewn" into the brain of man by repeated hammering as axioms (the so-called unproved truths). The main mass of undemanding philistine minds, by virtue of their ignorance, "falls for" the cheap installation and propaganda of following the imposed way and mode of life. The capitalist world, saturated through and through with lies, hypocrisy, double standards, simply does not know how to live otherwise (i.e., without taking "cream" under cover). And as for the mass media, they always take the position of the one from whose hands they are fed, which by no means testifies in favor of their independence; the mass media are still "party", as they were a hundred and two hundred years ago. The question of the purification of today's mass media from the filth of lies and half-truths is not solvable, apparently, outside the clarification of the personal positions of the representatives of these mass media. This question must be posed approximately in this way: will you be able, are you vouchsafed personally, as a subject form (as a journalist, correspondent, commentator, political scientist, etc.), to be a form-carrier and continuer of the developing objective content in opposition to the "fake", lying, venal subject forms that are present and rooted here today? Of course, this question makes sense only in conjunction with the solution of the problem of the proper upbringing of young cadres. Journalistic cadres, like medical ones, must be brought to an oath or a certain vow, proceeding from the understanding — of how serious a weapon against humanity the propagandized lies and inadequacy of thinking can be. The collective West, in the person of the overseas "hegemon" and its henchmen, has in fact already "played out" to the conviction that no objective content, no truth or verity exists in principle; everything is exhausted by general validity and by what can be imposed on the undemanding majority by the manipulation of human consciousness and by frankly force pressure. The manipulation of the consciousness of man — this is a crime against the person and against humanity. And the deliberate lie, and the sincere delusion, and all the more the manipulation of consciousness — are equally ruinous for the fates of humanity. The Western mass media seriously discuss the question of the onset of the epoch of the so-called "post-truth". The "post-truth" of an infinite number of subjective opinions and utterances (fictions, hardly distinguishable from truth; unfounded assertions, "appointed" to be truth; retold rumors and idle conjectures) must be urgently refuted by the proof of the absence of a stable direct and reverse connection, of succession and conjugacy of the contents of the injected subjective abstract forms with the objective content of the thing reality, of concrete facts, of reliably verified abstract logical forms with confirmed truthfulness. One cannot arbitrarily and unceremoniously deal with truth (with verity), and not because it is punishable by a certain "supreme deity" or "providence", but because the world is arranged dialectically. The return to the initially rejected forms of greater contentfulness will take place sooner or later, and

all the false, momentarily advantageous orientations together with their propagandists and customers will be rejected and overthrown. Such is the dialectical nature of the world, implying the inevitable return to itself, but every time at a more and more contentful (genuine, true, actual) level. The universal principle of self-development "governs" everything and everyone, manages all processes without exception, and so simply, on any crooked goat, it can no longer be bypassed. Everyone is free to choose himself — to accept this principle and follow it in life, or to deny and ignore it with the inevitable consequences for himself. The real state of affairs is such that, by virtue of this very dialectical nature of the surrounding world, everything sooner or later has the habit of returning "to the starting point", but not in its former form, but in other, more weighty and more contentful forms. In case of non-understanding or ignoring of such a layout, or in case of unskillful and imprudent dealing with the mentioned renewed forms — the impacts, and even the blows of these "weighted" returning forms may fall not where it is below the belt, but where it is above the neck. The growing contentlessness of the collective West will ruin it. And this will happen not by reason of God's punishment, God's providence, or the execution of a certain "law of karma", but because the exhausting contentfulness of their main everyday forms (of the way of life), based on human vices, provokes the absorption of these forms by considerably more contentful stable forms. Too many empty (low-contentful) form-formations at all levels have been generated by their mercantile aim and rampant individualism (sham post-industrial "economies"; the unsecuredness of the Fed currency; lying mass media; fruitless same-sex marriages; pretentious idle celebrations; the perverted "post-truth" "appointed as truth"; ostentatious values; pharisaical juvenile justice; the hypocritical multiculturalism, tolerance, political correctness that appeared out of nowhere, etc.). When a society (or a state) pursues low-contentful goals, then it is absorbed by more contentful and balanced social arrangements. The form-formations enumerated above (in parentheses) belong to abstract constructions and structures, invented by an extremely subjectivized consciousness and erected on shaky (false) unnatural foundations. These constructions and structures, generated by the subjective imagination in the minds of a few individuals, do not belong to the number of those tested by time and confirmed by customs and traditions, but have in their arsenal only a limited number of more or less successfully realized examples. Here we directly encounter the manifestation of voluntarism, with the arbitrary absolutization of the subjective component in the known systemic relation {subjective/objective}, which is fraught with the violation of the balance or equilibrium of the whole relation as a whole. Too small a volume of factual material is at the disposal of man to verify and confirm the truthfulness of the abstract constructions of the indicated type and to admit them then as

new foundations for mass application by humanity. Without a thorough assessment of the contentfulness of the fruits of the inflamed brain and unbridled fantasies by the procedures of practice and interpretation, the truthfulness of these forms, to put it mildly, raises doubts, and the stubborn universal inculcation — is certainly dangerous. In every relation similar to the relation {change/basis}, a typical representative of which is also the relation {subjective/objective} examined by us, there is present a definite correlation of the components "change" and "basis" in their relation, guaranteeing the stability and steady development of the relation itself as a whole at one or another stage of the development of reality. The finding of such a correlation of the components-constituents in the indicated relations, corresponding to a certain averaged balanced value, determined and conditioned by every given stage of the development of reality, always has been and will be the most important priority task standing both before individual subjects and before their communities. As soon as the immature subjective thinking begins to ignore its connection with the "down-to-earth" levels of the basis, so it goes into an extreme, into a one-sidedness, into the absolutization of this one-sidedness. Thinking speculatively and practically boundlessly can manipulate everything whatsoever, which it does, and obtains any "results", and achieves "outstanding" successes. But much of these "achievements" is acquired at the price of the loss of the adequacy of the representations about reality. And in this case the individual or community, recklessly and "crazy-headedly" using the huge potential of thinking (characterized by the high intensity of content-form transformations), fall into a trap. Quite in the spirit of the Anglo-Saxon subjectivized tradition — by force to drive into "paradise" and to "cultivate" (civilize, democratize, etc.) all the disagreeing and resisting. Such, from time to time repeated, neglect of the objectively taking-shape situation will inevitably return to the Anglo-Saxons as a boomerang of punishments for their new generations, which will have to "reverse gear", paying for the inadequacy and low-contentfulness of their ancestors. Unfortunately, the majority (and, first of all, the youth) does not see this yet, naively believing that the technocratic policy and technological "advancement" will automatically ensure primacy and advantage in all spheres of human activity. In reality, however, the ugly one-sidedness or imbalance in the development of different sides-constituents of individual civilizations leads the latter to degeneration into their opposite and to inevitable collapse. [As a result of the excessive rocking or loss of equilibrium, the "ship of civilization", speaking figuratively, capsizes upside down, i.e., the situation turns into its opposite: in the place where man was (the so-called crown of nature, according to the very widespread opinion nowadays), there turns out to be a bottom soaked with tar, and the crown of nature is swallowed by the abyss, i.e., returns into the wild (thing) nature and is assimilated by it.]

After all, what mainly distinguishes the modern Western ideology from the Eastern one (in particular, within the framework of the antagonism USA vs Russia)? These are different representations about the necessary and sufficient correlation of the components in the relation {change/basis} (or in the most general relation {content/form}). The Western world makes the accent on the prevalence of the constituent of change (transcendent enterprise, infinite freedom, unlimited individualism and egoism, etc.), whereas the East gives preference to the component of the basis — stability, experience, common sense, justified conservatism, collectivism. And already from here, on the part of the West, follows the neglect of morality, ethics, humanism. Here two different approaches meet and compete, relying on different specific weights (including in digital expression) of the abovementioned components-constituents in their inseparable integrity. For the finding of the balance or the golden mean between the indicated components, of course, both a reserve of patience and an ever more meaningful loyalty of the West and the East with respect to each other are needed. However, no less significant is also the understanding that at times it is simply necessary to actively counteract aggression (both in the air, and at sea, and on land, and, especially, in space). In the trenches, alternately turning the cheeks for blows, one cannot sit out. One should more boldly and energetically bloc with like-minded people and by collective efforts control the balance of the system {West/East}. In particular, all the "iron" of opaque and doubtful purpose, launched into space by the "hegemon", must find its rest at the bottom of the world ocean. There must be no illusions; one cannot fail to see that in this case the West represents not so much an active as an aggressive component of the relation {change/basis}, opposing the moderately-active component of the East. The West is "commanded" by a quite different principle of reality than the East, and the full compatibility or coincidence of both principles is not achievable in principle. That is why the unsafe algorithms "swarming" in the heads of the collective West must be neutralized at the earliest stages. The known inadequacy of a monkey with a grenade can lead to the collapse of the whole system {West/East} and to the departure "into non-being" of such a formlevel of reality as humanity. It goes without saying that the development of reality and the natural universal course of events under the "control" of the principle of self-development cannot be stopped by this single act — simply a separate link in the general chain of development will be transformed into a new form, which will continue its contentful movement at a new level, but already without man. However, to man himself his fate, perhaps, is not so indifferent. The procedure of finding the balance of the above-mentioned componentsconstituents is complicated also by the fact that the components are subject to the permanent influence of numerous external and internal factors (i.e., of the same components of other relations), which makes them very mobile within the framework of a single relation and, in its turn, leads to the sporadic variability of the relation itself as a whole.

Changes without a basis (i.e., the denial and reduction to a minimum of the significance and role of the basis) in the known relation {change/basis} — this is absurd. The basis directs the changes, preserves them in the form of layers and thereby sets the vector of the subsequent changes (i.e., of development). The changes that become isolated and "tear away" from the basis liquidate the relation as a whole as such, since something, being deprived of the basis and "not remembering kinship", is transformed into its opposite. The vector of development is to a considerable degree determined by the basis, i.e., by the past, by the "lived-through", by the preceding accumulations. The role of Russia as the main stabilizing component or counterbalance in the system {West/East}, threatening at times to go to pieces, — is indisputable. True, the most effectively this counterbalance will function only on the basis of the renewed social structure with the socialist order, which, undoubtedly, is our national priority and pride for centuries. The absence (especially among politicians) of adherence to such, for example, qualities of man and indicators of the developed harmonious society as honor, conscience, decency, morality, ethics, philanthropy, mutual respect — is a direct path "into non-being" for those human communities which refuse such humanistic acquisitions, ideals, and convictions. The inadequate behavior of the majority of individuals (of society), as a reaction to the movement into nowhere, "sanctified" by contentless goals, is punished by "non-being", i.e., by the absorption and assimilation of the lowcontentful forms by the healthy adequate forms. Russia, with its considerably more adequate than that of the collective West representations about reality, has every chance for leadership with a humanistic orientation, provided that a way is found to transform the ugly and defective capitalist forms imposed on it into other harmonious forms of development. As time and the course of the developing events show, the subjective, it would seem, question of the justice of the social structure [Roman law, for example, refers the notion of justice to the subjective categories] turns out to be a question not at all subjective, but exclusively objective. Taking into account the striving and craving for justice originally inherent in our people, the following ideological scheme or chain of contentful transformations has quite matured for embodiment at a new level: truth (verity) => justice =>law =>equality => (social) equilibrium =>balance =>harmony ... Any human community or civilization in one way or another (explicitly and implicitly) strives for the realization of such a chain, conditioned by the dialectical nature of the surrounding world. In reality — this is a question of life and death for all the human communities presently existing. It is worth, perhaps, to dwell separately on the role and significance of modern advertising.

Today's aggressive advertising on TV — this is, as a rule, well-paid lie and deliberately distorted representation of the true state of affairs. In the language of advertising, from the tenderest nails, a signal is sent to man: one can lie a lot and with impunity, from a high tribune and to a multi-million audience. Advertising almost always peremptorily hypertrophies the limited positive and advantage of the goods, services, and all sorts of "brands", hiding in the backyard their negative features and shortcomings. In this consists its lie, by this it perniciously influences the unformed young minds. The lying advertising from morning till evening teaches the youth also to treat easily and undemandingly their own unfulfilled promises and empty assurances. Advertising must be removed from TV unambiguously. The most "creative" liars cry out that without advertising there will be no TV. They lie, shamelessly lie, for the principles of the functioning of TV are deliberately distorted and disfigured by the dealers from TV. Those lie who have a relation to the huge money that revolves in this backstage and that can be uncontrollably stuffed into pockets. In the conditions of the social character of production and the private-capitalist mode of appropriation, the throwing of dust in the eyes of the respectable public — is an inalienable element of all television shows and attractions. When the matter concerns the distribution and appropriation of the product worked out under capitalism, everyone must immediately forget about the transparency of these procedures — otherwise by no forces will it be possible to gather on relatively tiny patches the transcendent number of crooks and other odious individuals. Meanwhile, the way around these "naturally" organized difficulties is quite well-known and artless — to create an accumulation fund, public consumption funds and to feed with them the vital and demanded directions, and not only on TV; this is, perhaps, so far the most effective and tested way of the development of human initiatives in the conditions of the coming socialist order. Let us touch upon the background of the origin and rooting of false premises. A lie — this is also a certain contentful form, but with an extremely low degree of objective contentfulness, i.e., a form of a very distant (rough) approximation to the expression of the objective content of reality (a lowcontentful, and therefore untruthful form). A shameless lie — this is a factual refusal of the moral-ethical norms and categories in favor of a temporary, momentary, as a rule, material gain (with its single measure — the volume of the money supply). To such a choice is brought by the cultivation of the vividly expressed individualism in opposition to collectivism and the priority of public interests. The inability and unwillingness to distinguish the inessential or little-essential from the essential, firstly, and the arbitrary absolutization of this little-

essential to the detriment of the essential, secondly, — is akin to an impudent shameless lie. Using such a device one can for a long time effectively make mountains out of molehills, deliberately belittling the contentfulness of the essential forms. The conscious belittling of the essential and the exaltation of the inessential is equivalent to the mixing of lie with half-truth, and therefore this is the most refined, vile, and disgusting variety of lie. However, sooner or later the natural installation on the growth of the contentfulness of the forms will take its toll and will arrange the forms in their places according to the level of their contentfulness. Every lie — this is a deliberate attempt to lead aside and to evade the adequate thinking. When we are tried to be convinced that black is white, referring to the truthfulness of the generally valid (i.e., to the opinion of a certain "little collective" that has gathered under its banners a limited number of individuals), then these propagandists of the generally valid to the detriment of the objective must bear in mind that the answer for the imposition of a failed opinion may, as it may turn out, have to be held not before the hoodwinked "little collective", but before the deceived humanity. Virtual reality — this is a lie based on the unsupported and unconfirmed by the content of concrete forms admissibility of the prevalence of the subjective constituent in the relation {subjective/objective} to the detriment of the objective constituent. The modern cinematography of the "overseas" Hollywood type (which has splendidly mastered the "Solzhenitsyn" motives and devices of inflating the less essential to scales surpassing everything essential) is saturated with fictions, tricks, and special effects, hindering the adequate perception of reality. The indulgence of the "overseas" inadequacy again is fraught with consequences for the whole world. In the final account, this is the right of every individual to choose — in which direction and to what extent one should shift the dividing line in the relation {subjective/objective}, but, fortunately, the criterion of the correctness and admissibility of such "shifts" nevertheless exists; this is the adequacy of the content of thinking to the content of being. In the case of the absolutization of the component "subjective", the harmless, at first glance, inadequacy of thinking and of the formed representations about reality can lead to serious consequences. The above-mentioned "little collective" or a certain limited community and its collective consciousness is capable of existing in the "fake" regime of the inadequate perception of reality only for a very limited time as well, but even in a short period it can create a global threat to the survival of humanity as a whole, and even destroy it ... Let us cite a somewhat hyperbolized and therefore quite obvious for comprehension example. Suppose, from the apartment of the seventh floor there are two exits outside: through the door and through the window. Everyone knows that one can go out both through the door and through the

window, but the overwhelming majority habitually uses the door, since it knows that the exit through the window is fraught with grave consequences. And nevertheless there are separate individuals who, following obsessive ideas, leave the apartment through the window, and for one reason or another their number will never be reduced to zero. The realization of such an alternative possibility on the scale of the whole humanity would threaten the disappearance of humanity as a species. The loss of the adequacy of the representations about reality in this case could turn out to be critical and be expressed in the loss of humanity as a definite level of reality. The speech here is precisely about such a "can" which "cannot" from the point of view of the adequately thinking man (and of humanity as a whole). [Deliberately exaggerated examples sometimes help to feel the real danger of flirting with inadequacy]. Another example. The Ptolemaic picture of the world proceeded from the primacy of the geocentric representations. Was this inadequacy of representations critical for those times? Probably, no. But with time, little by little, it became such. In any case, no accuracy of the calculation of the trajectory of the flight of space vehicles on the basis of the Ptolemaic model (because of the transcendent cumbersomeness and error of the calculations) could be out of the question today. Thinking easily shifts the boundary in the relation {subjective/objective} in the direction of the prevalence of the subjective (for itself, as for the most abstracted level of reality, this is not critical), but at the same time it can, to the detriment of adequacy, provoke the "exit" of the values of the whole relation beyond the limits of the range admissible by this definite stage of development. In other words, for "proper" thinking some inadequate preferences and unbridled internal activity (while they are localized in the head, without exit outside) can remain and without consequences, but for the whole linkage-aggregate of the levels of man (dwelling in the thing world and including in itself the form of thingness, the form of sensations, and the form of thinking) — the arbitrary choice of thinking with the subsequent exit of the inadequate reactions outside can turn out to be critical, which is shown by the examples cited above. A lie is often dictated by the striving to adapt to the external conditions and influences in the name of the survival and prosperity of some separate formlevel in isolation from the remaining levels of the general aggregate in the composition of the structure of man (say, the exaltation of the flesh and material acquisitions to the detriment or bypass of morality and ethics). At the same time, at the mental level there "reigns" a factual "dual power" (split): something more formal collides with something more contentful. As a result, a conflict arises: the contentful constituent "supervises", as a rule, directly the straightforward, artless path of the achievement of the goal; the formal constituent, however, for the sake of the achievement of the goal, as it seems, by a more sure-fire path, is ready to go roundabout, i.e., to lie.

Truth and lie — forms of deliberately different degrees of approximation to the expression of the fullness of the moving (objective) content. By virtue of the perniciousness of the influence of even the smallest public "fakes" and fictions on the minds and consciousness of people, one must fight against lie harshly, resolutely, and mercilessly — to suppress, not leaving without attention a single serious attempt of the rooting of falsity. When the question arises about the survival of humanity or of its separate communities, the orientation to the inadequate thinking, false premises, and "values" not approved by life is categorically inadmissible. In order not to find oneself on the sidelines of development, the contentfulness of all without exception forms (ideas, perspective plans, projects, programs, directions, etc.) planned on the state scale for development and embodiment must be analyzed and ranked at the state and public levels in a permanent and uninterrupted regime. However, not only mean, petty, or mercenary motives and goals are the background of the fake injections and the spread of lies. Their "feasible" contribution of confusion to the public life is made by the humanitarians, who, by virtue of their professional orientation to the world of people and the sphere of psychology, live in isolation from the physical world and are easily intoxicated by the ruling "top", poorly imagining how bread grows, steel is boiled, and energy is released at the fission of the uranium nucleus. The widening illiteracy in the questions of natural science — also one of the causes of the false representations about the world, of the spread and tenacity of myths. The unprincipledness of the position and the absence of firm convictions among the statesmen and ordinary citizens in a similar way is akin to the renunciation of truth and the patronage of lie. The "comprador guts" of the powers that be and of the so-called business — again from the same series. The very widespread opinion on that score that the market allegedly will put everything in its places, both in the sphere of production and consumption, — also a lie or at least a dense delusion. If already by chance, unexpectedly (well, they overlooked, "missed", with whom it does not happen!) there arose man with his thinking (and, correspondingly, everything connected with him, the so-called "artificial"), then, most likely, he in one way or another consciously and unconsciously will interfere and in the nature of all things (or this is already generally beyond the limits of understanding?). Getting burned and making mistakes and learning on them, man will constantly correct both his own life and the influence of the external environment — another path of development, except for the "making of oneself from oneself", simply does not exist. For this reason both the state planning, and the state support, and the state regulation in various fields of production and consumption are quite regular and will certainly be present as

a consequence of the vision of the processes of development by the eyes of the subject (the initiator of everything "artificial"). Another matter is that with all this the question remains as before topical, which also no one is able to cancel and which the subjective consciousness will have to solve permanently in the name of the provision of the steady contentful development — the finding of the optimal correlation or balance of the mutually complementary components of the "invisible hand of the market" and the "state regulation" (i.e., the balance or optimal correlation of the artificial and the natural). And, finally, one cannot fail to see today how destructive a weapon (a weapon of mass destruction) the information pandemic can turn out to be and how important it is to be able to verify and establish the truthfulness of the information injections. In such a connection, the significance and role of the technology of interpretation (in solving the problem of the establishment of truth) as a means of counteracting the threat of the infliction on humanity of all kinds of damage of a global scale by means of cunningly planned information attacks increase immeasurably. Unfortunately, we do not have the possibility to develop this theme further here. Everywhere, at all levels of the development of reality, between all its forms of all levels without exception there takes place a harsh competitive struggle for the possession of the highest contentfulness or the struggle for the contentful resource, which turns out to be a direct consequence of the universality of the principle of self-development. The striving to the expression of truth and verity, determined by this same principle, is originally inherent, including, in the very "mechanism" of human thinking (consciousness). And if today a certain lie is considered an ordinary and habitual matter and is accepted "with a bang" by undemanding collectives and communities, then tomorrow there will certainly be found a subject (or subjects) who, obeying the immanent principle of self-development present in him, will "dig up" the truth about this falsity, and all the constructions erected on lying premises will collapse in an instant, and even with the attendant consequences for those who erected the lie on a pedestal. Moreover, such a scenario is realized by no means by reason of the intervention of "higher forces", but under the "pressure" of the objectively everywhere present universal, unconditional, blind, and relative principle of self-development with its "installations" on the possession of the maximally achievable contentfulness by all forms-levels of the changing reality. Conclusions The above set forth we consider a sufficient basis for the following conclusions. 1. The objective reality represents the movement and change of its forms in the direction of the possession by the mentioned forms of the highest

contentfulness. The cognitive process can be more effective if the development of reality is examined from the position of the content-form transformations of unitary directionality. 2. The human consciousness (thinking) — this is the most abstracted (of the reliably known today) form of the representation of the content of reality, continuing by itself the series of the preceding forms, such as the form of thingness and the form of sensations. Getting rid of the representations about consciousness as an "reflection" or "copy" of reality, human thinking acquires the possibility to see the development of reality as a single ("through") movement of its content by means of ever more abstracting contentful forms; thereby the artificial gnoseological barrier is eliminated, erected by the human consciousness itself on the path of the cognition of the contentfully infinite (multilevel) movement of reality. 3. The genuine criterion of truth or of the truthfulness of the forms of the developing reality is established. The truthfulness of this or that form of reality is determined by the fullness or the "quantity" of its contentfulness. Hence, the criterion of the truthfulness of the forms of reality is served by their contentfulness. 4. The above-mentioned criterion makes it possible to quantitatively assess the contentfulness of the forms of reality, which gives the possibility to arrange them by rank and thereby more precisely and more meaningfully determine the direction of the perspective investigations. 5. Practice is not the criterion of truth, but serves as a procedure (technology, way) of the assessment of the fullness or the "quantity" of the contentfulness of all sorts of forms of reality. In the indicated sense practice represents a particular case of the more general technology, named interpretation. 6. The essence of the process of cognition is reduced to the fact that everything investigated and uncognized is assessed, verified, and expressed through the available and existing, the truthfulness of which has already been established and verified by time. 7. There do not exist two or more equally contentful truths (ideas, representations, assertions, theories, conceptions, models, opinions, points of view, etc.) with respect to one and the same investigated question or subject of judgment, just as there cannot be in principle two or more equivalent approximations to the objective truth or to the absolute truth. At any given moment of development among the forms of different approximations there exists only one form, the most approximated to the form of the objective (absolute) contentfulness. This is precisely the form on which one should orient oneself as on the true one. References

Unlearned Lessons of Dialectics: From Whom and How to Learn to Think

Unlearned Lessons of Dialectics: From Whom and How to Learn to Think Dialectically Abstract This article is dedicated to the lessons of dialectics from Vladimir I. Lenin — an outstanding national thinker to whom the ability of clear dialectical thinking was generously gifted by nature. Any work that came from Lenin's pen is imbued with the spirit of dialectics. Any Lenin's thought is a chain or series of conceptual forms maximally filled with objective content. Any of his theoretical work is a model of masterfully balanced sliding of thought as if on the "razor's edge", the opposite sides-facets of which symbolize the confrontation of two non-identical opposing sides-components of reality. The aim of this article is to draw the attention primarily of the young reader precisely to these qualities of Vladimir I. Lenin's theoretical works. In the course of familiarizing oneself with Lenin's theoretical heritage, the circumstance that the research approach he used remains unclaimed to this day, and Lenin's lessons of dialectics, accordingly, remain unlearned, catches the eye. Since at the foundation of any certain form of reality (object, process, phenomenon) lie two highly abstract and non-identical, yet at the same time objective and interrelated constituent sides-components "content" and "form", the content-form relations (conversions, transformations) come to the fore and become the subject of close attention of dialectics. Considering Lenin's theoretical works through the prism of content-form relations and identifying on this basis the general regularities of the construction and presentation of material makes it possible to speak about the possibility of creating a single universal methodology for conducting effective research at any level and in any field of reality. Introduction

As an epigraph to this article, we would like to take words by Vladimir I. Lenin from his work "On the Road", written in 1909: "...this stage must be outgrown; the new circumstances of the moment require new forms of struggle..." [8, p. 540]. The cited quote, in our view, vividly expresses not only the character of a remarkable living and flexible personality but also the essence of the entire life and revolutionary activity of V.I. Lenin: keenly and relentlessly tracking the situation, accurately grasping its changes, and being constantly ready to respond to these changes with the most adequate actions — new forms of struggle. In this article, we will examine and analyze the greatness and scale of Lenin's figure from the standpoint of his innate ability: to be a dialectician. Discarding all fictional, doubtful and secondary elements that were thrust into the foreground during the "murky" 1990s — years of merciless reprisals against Soviet power and the USSR — we will focus mainly on this aspect of Lenin's activities and personality in our research. A dialectical mindset is that indispensable trait of Lenin's individuality which, in our opinion, has not yet received due coverage in fiction and journalism. All other attributes of genius are already derivatives of such outstanding features of his nature: adherence to a scientific approach in studying any issues; striving for objective and impartial presentation of conclusions and results obtained through observations and studies, etc. It was precisely Lenin's natural "immersion" in the mysterious medium of dialectics that provoked fierce hostility and rejection towards him from his enemies and opponents who, lacking this natural gift, could boast of nothing more than inventing plots "out of their heads" and presenting scholastic twists, assumptions, fables and fairy tales. Another reason for pathological hatred and animosity toward Lenin's personality lies in the inability of his opponents to achieve a penetrating, Lenin-like insight into the essence and order of things; and this unattainable depth of understanding actual relations remains the main irritant for his opponents even now. What is the strength of Lenin, if even his most die-hard detractors and adversaries nevertheless recognize his influence and authority on a global scale? First of all, in our view, it lies in his philosophical significance and specifically in mastery of dialectics: when foresight is the result of a talent of an individual to see the development of reality through the prism of interaction between directly opposing sides of this (reality). He was what many ordinary and extraordinary philosophizing individuals secretly desire and dream of becoming, realizing however that they fall short, do not measure up, and cannot become anyone similar — because some extremely important constituent elements are missing in the multi-layered structure of their own "I". It is difficult to find a modern public leader who stands at least level with (on the same step) Lenin — an encyclopedic scholar and philosopher of profound depth,

a brilliant polemicist, consistent and fearless revolutionary, prominent politician and outstanding statesman. Possession of the invaluable gift of a born dialectician allowed Lenin to handle events and circumstances with exquisite precision, balancing on the crest of the wave. The accuracy and precision with which historical figures of a dialectical "leaven" walk along the "razor's edge", balancing between Scylla and Charybdis in interpreting the questions under study, evokes admiration and envy in the best sense of the word. Through them (and via them), dialectics charts its way most effectively and optimally. It is precisely through them and their voices that dialectically developing nature speaks to us. It is regrettable, of course, that the beauty and perfection of thought, say, of Marx or Engels can be appreciated by most of us only thanks to the efforts of translators. Undoubtedly, those translators of the time were highly qualified specialists and quite exceptional people, but as someone aptly noted, reading classics in translation is like taking a shower while wearing a coat. And so much the better that the beauty, clarity and precision of Lenin's thought can, thank god, be known and evaluated without the interference of intermediary translators. All people (especially outstanding ones) are to one degree or another spontaneous dialecticians. And it does not matter what craft (of mental or physical labor) they possess. Dialectics, ingrained in the flesh and blood of every person, occasionally orients him/her towards certain paradoxical (it seems, from the point of view of common sense) solutions, thanks to which he/she achieves remarkable results and therefore becomes famous among the general public. We refer here, for example, to memoirs and recollections of celebrated personalities: Marshal Zhukov, pilots Pokryshkin and Kozhedub, aircraft engine designer Mikulin, director Eisenstein, cardiac surgeon Amosov, weightlifter Vlasov and many others whose innate art of paradoxical dialectical thinking helped them "squeeze through" literally through the eye of a needle and win unpredictable victories, make discoveries, achieve unexpected and unforeseen success in seemingly unfavorable situations according to everyday consciousness. But Lenin was by no means a spontaneous latent dialectician, whom there are countless numbers of on planet Earth; rather, like Hegel, Marx and Engels, he undoubtedly felt and realized himself as a dialectician and acted accordingly to the dialectical nature of the world and the set of tools provided by this nature: not inventing tools out of one's head, but discovering them using one's head [16, p. 148]. Accordingly, any conclusion drawn by Lenin is not arbitrariness or fabrication, but a sober judgment based on careful study and meticulous analysis of the established situation with identification of all the "actors and performers". As mentioned above, through thinkers such as Marx, Hegel, Engels, Lenin, nature itself (the surrounding world, developing reality) talks to us in the language of pure dialectics. And our Vladimir Ilyich looks in this row of

dialecticians even "cooler" than the aforementioned Germans, especially in matters of practical implementation and embodiment of theoretical developments. Not only "Philosophical Notebooks" (compiled from quotes and notes made in margins of books read) or “Materialism and Empiriocriticism” serve as evidence of the author's deep understanding of the dialectical foundations of building and harmony of the surrounding world, but any of his other works regardless of content, and even solely on formal grounds, confirm his belonging to the ranks of true conductors and tribunes of dialectics. Why does Lenin's figure attract such close and lasting attention? Because he is one of the greatest dialecticians of all times and peoples — a direct successor and multiplier of the dialectical legacy of Hegel, Marx, Engels. Lenin is so great and significant in terms of the practical application of the mastery of dialectical thinking that none of the scientists and philosophers of all stripes who lived after him and live today can currently be placed alongside him in this respect. Brilliants of such size and cut appear once in hundreds of years. Completely distancing ourselves from the heap of lies and absurdities expressed about this outstanding thinker during the so-called "time of trouble" at the end of the last century (let this remain entirely on the conscience of talkative simpletons), we will assess the scale of Lenin's personality from the perspective of the spirit and atmosphere of his theoretical works freely available: please, feel free to read, verify, adopt or refute what we have presented — depending on your comprehension and preparation. With the expectation that this article may provide some support in mastering the art of dialectical thinking (which is especially important for students of both humanities and technical universities), we will demonstrate and reveal, using Lenin's works as examples, the beauty and harmony of the dialectical method in opposition to mechanistic, metaphysical, eclectic and scholastic ways of thinking. Ultimately, what is important to know when studying various forms of reality: object, process, phenomenon, etc.? It is essential to identify and track the logic, historicism, trend, patterns, direction (i.e., the contentful side) of the development of these forms of manifestation and existence of reality, for which it turns out to be absolutely necessary to observe how transformation (transmutation, conversion) of one form of reality into another takes place, and furthermore, on this basis, try to forecast such transformations (transformations, conversions), anticipating not only the change of existing forms but also the emergence of new forms. This constitutes the subject matter of dialectics. Ultimately, it comes down to studying the surrounding world (nature, man, society) from the position of content-form transformations, transmutations, conversions [2, 3, 7, 18].

Every serious researcher, embarking on research work, proceeds from the premise that reality (the surrounding world, nature) is always at least dual-sided (i.e., double-faceted). Any real specific object, process or phenomenon in its most general abstract form represents a relation of two principles (sides, constituents, components, "opponents" etc.): {content/form}. Consequently, any concrete form of reality (implying: object, process, phenomenon) is determined by the correlation of these noted sides-constituents-components within each of their specific relations. The development of forms of reality itself represents changes in the correlations of these constituent-components within their relations.
